**Result: sys.ts itself recorded 0 events and 0 distinct `(rule, site, words)` triples. The closure recorded 5,267 events and 3,751 distinct triples. Declaration stopped at `src/compiler/core.ts:261:1`; sys.ts body and runtime lowering remain unknown. This measurement cannot establish that the rest of sys.ts can be staffed as a complete wall list.**

## Reproduction and retained evidence

The [scan-all README](../scan-all/README.md) was read in full before creating the worktree. The commands used the repository Go 1.27.1 toolchain; all five requested scratch variables were set outside the worktree. The wrapper copies stdout only and preserves pipeline failure. There was exactly one sys.ts probe invocation; controls and verification have their own fixture invocations.

```sh
git -C /workspace/adamic fetch origin 03c140ec28a249b8b0f13eb24e34fc3d3df05266
git -C /workspace/adamic worktree add --detach /workspace/scratch/scan-all-sys-03c140ec/repo 03c140ec28a249b8b0f13eb24e34fc3d3df05266
cd /workspace/scratch/scan-all-sys-03c140ec/repo
git submodule update --init --recursive --depth 1
(cd stage3/api && npm ci)
git clone --depth 1 --branch v6.0.3 https://github.com/microsoft/TypeScript.git /workspace/scratch/scan-all-sys-03c140ec/typescript
git -C /workspace/scratch/scan-all-sys-03c140ec/typescript rev-parse HEAD
export PATH="/workspace/adamic-tools/bin:/workspace/adamic-tools/go/bin:$PATH"
export ADAMIC_TYPESCRIPT_SOURCE=/workspace/scratch/scan-all-sys-03c140ec/typescript
export GOTMPDIR=/workspace/scratch/scan-all-sys-03c140ec/gotmp
export TMPDIR=/workspace/scratch/scan-all-sys-03c140ec/tmp
export GOCACHE=/workspace/scratch/scan-all-sys-03c140ec/gocache
export ADAMIC_BUILD_CACHE_DIR=/workspace/scratch/scan-all-sys-03c140ec/buildcache
export SCAN_ALL_SCRATCH=/workspace/scratch/scan-all-sys-03c140ec/run
bash stage3/census/scan-all/verify.sh > "$SCAN_ALL_SCRATCH/verify.log" 2>&1
stage3/apply.sh --build-product "$SCAN_ALL_SCRATCH/adapted" > "$SCAN_ALL_SCRATCH/apply.log" 2>&1
export SCAN_ALL_REAL_BINARY=/workspace/scratch/scan-all-sys-03c140ec/buildcache/local/bf3b054ed0ada2d09261170b714c310f80bccb247ef12fe12513c3898b07accb/probe
export SCAN_ALL_BINARY="$SCAN_ALL_SCRATCH/capture-probe.sh"
python3 stage3/census/scan-all/report.py "$SCAN_ALL_BINARY" \
  "$SCAN_ALL_SCRATCH/adapted/src/compiler/sys.ts" "$SCAN_ALL_SCRATCH/sys.md" \
  > "$SCAN_ALL_SCRATCH/report.log" 2>&1
```

Capture wrapper:

```sh
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail
"${SCAN_ALL_REAL_BINARY:?}" "$@" 2>"${SCAN_ALL_SCRATCH:?}/sys.stderr" | tee "$SCAN_ALL_SCRATCH/sys.raw.json"
```
